COMMENT     VALIDATED REFSEQ: This record has undergone validation or
            preliminary review. This record has been curated by Dr. Andrew
            Davison (MRC Virology Unit, Glasgow, UK). The reference sequence
            was derived from AJ004801.
            ACCURACY: Potential sequence errors are noted under CDS features.
            CURATION: The original gene nomenclature has been retained. Genes
            presumably inherited from the common ancestor of alpha-, beta- and
            gammaherpesviruses (core genes) and non-core genes presumably
            inherited from the ancestor of alphaherpesviruses (alpha genes) are
            indicated. Initiation codons are assigned with as much confidence
            as is possible for each protein-coding region. A standard protein
            nomenclature has been applied so that orthologs have the same name
            in all herpesviruses. Protein information may have been propagated
            from other herpesvirus species.
            COMPLETENESS: full length.
